## Operation status handover / archival rule

**While an operation is active**, `PROJECT-STATUS.md` displays the full discovered-source/serializer register for that operation, with each newly found source path logged **before** implementation and test updates recorded as they occur. New discoveries are allowed and must appear with a precise status immediately.

**When O06 is truly ACCEPTED** (all G1–G6 accepted with source-backed evidence and DEV/PRD verification): preserve the complete O06 source register, status history, test journals and logs as permanent repository records. Before removing the expanded O06 section from this page, archive its final Markdown snapshot under `catalog/compliance/archive/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.md` and retain the canonical JSON `UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json` without deleting records. Replace the entire O06 live register on this page with **one compact O06 ACCEPTED row**, including final commit, acceptance date and links to the archived source inventory and verification evidence. **Never mark O06 complete or hide unresolved statuses before acceptance.** Disable O06's automatic injection into this page once archived; further O06 regression results belong in its archival/evidence files unless they cause reopening.

Then reserve the live detail area for **O07**, using a separate O07 register and new discoveries, with the same update-before-work/test-by-test discipline. Repeat for subsequent operations. This page remains an active-operation dashboard; the detailed source/test archive remains accessible in Git.

## O06 live source serializer register

**Canonical recovery reference:** this table is updated during discovery, mapping, implementation, tests and deployment, not retrospectively.

Found source entries: **294** (R82=26, R83=26, R96=26, U86=49, U97=49, D97=7, C98=50, C227=5, T99=56).

**Current per-entry status:** EXCLUDED=38, MAPPED=256.

**States:** `DISCOVERED` = located in Epic source only; `MAPPED` = source behavior/reader mapped; `PARTIAL` = a subset of the source-defined serializer is implemented/tested, but remaining source-branch verification or code fixes are unresolved; `GAP` = confirmed missing implementation; `BLOCKED` = named prerequisite; `EXCLUDED` = proven non-saveable or runtime-only; `DEV_PASS`/`PRD_PASS` = explicit source-scoped named tests passed at that target; `ACCEPTED` = source contract signed off. An entry that has passed a narrow fixture but may have unresolved fields remains PARTIAL.

**Mandatory live workflow:** (1) On discovering a symbol, add it immediately as DISCOVERED with source location. (2) Update to MAPPED/PARTIAL/GAP when the reader/code is inspected, naming remaining work. (3) Record test script, evidence and exact outcome when run; change the same row before starting another task. (4) Commit/push/deploy the updated status with each bounded checkpoint. (5) Never turn a registration or a corpus-only result into serializer acceptance. A latest DEV/PRD PASS means only that the named fixture passed there; it NEVER means class acceptance while the status is PARTIAL. Check the Outstanding / evidence column for the precise unfinished work. Some PARTIAL entries need only verification; others still need code. When the complete source contract is demonstrated, promote that row explicitly to ACCEPTED with evidence. Never replace an older failure without retaining its journal. The O06 test runner records STARTED then PASS/FAIL here automatically, while leaving class-level acceptance unchanged. The six O06 gates remain fixed even if new rows are discovered.

Machine-readable file: `catalog/compliance/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json`. Regenerate/update using `python catalog/bin/sync-ue1-o06-serializers.py` or `--set-status ID STATUS --reader ... --test ... --evidence ... --remaining ...`. Existing statuses survive rescans. Paths below are relative to their corresponding Epic source roots. **These rows are source sightings, not all distinct on-disk requirements.**

### O06 source-version expansion using local Graphify (2026-10-22)

**Discovered and registered immediately:** after indexing other UE1 versions, the live O06 register was extended with **106 source-version sightings**: v0.82 26 registrations (`R82`), v0.83 26 (`R83`), v0.86x 49 (`U86`), and v1.227 5 explicit `Serialize` definitions (`C227`). All 106 entries were recorded first as **DISCOVERED** with local Epic source path/line; 52 early-v9 sightings (R82/R83) were subsequently advanced to **MAPPED** after a reproducible byte-identity proof across 200/200 matching C/C++ source and header files (`tools/graphify/Compare-1996-Versions.py`, [evidence](evidence/o06/20261022-ue1-v9-v082-v083-source-identity.txt)). Both trees are the exact same code for the examined 200 files, so do not treat those sightings as 52 separate code implementation gaps. Their v9 UnrealDB field/reader parity still requires G1 reconciliation. The remaining 54 newer source sightings retain **DISCOVERED** status. Original 172 entries and recorded tests were preserved. The new rows are version-specific comparison work for G1/G2/G3/G4.

**Version facts checked against source:** v0.82 and v0.83 `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009`, v0.84a `0x000A`; v0.86x and v0.867 `UNREAL_FILE_VERSION=23`. Equal numeric versions do **not** prove that every serialized subclass layout stayed identical: compare actual native methods, headers and changed structures before mapping the new rows. The v1.227 source tree's provenance is not yet established, so `C227` sightings are discovery-only. No O06 completion gate was added, closed or falsely promoted.

PARTIAL means production implementation still has source-contract gaps. EXCLUDED means Epic does not persist that registration.

| Register state | Before | After this batch |
|---|---:|---:|
| PARTIAL | 197 | **126** |
| MAPPED | 57 | **128** |
| EXCLUDED | 38 | **38** |

The generic UDatabase base remains PARTIAL because it is not independently typed. **Second mapping group (12 entries):** both May and July UBitArray, UBitMatrix, UBspNodes, UBspSurfs, UMusic and USound. Source-defined native headers, branch-specific 55/56B BSP node fields, 44B surfaces, compressed bit arrays and DataSize audio payloads already have dedicated production readers; register includes precise Epic source and handler evidence. **Third mapping group (23 entries):** G1-accepted original 1996 saveable reader registrations R96, already source-mapped 23/23 with recorded G1 acceptance and deployed matching handlers. Their prior PARTIAL state represented future G5 testing/O10 linking, not unfinished G1 serialization. **Total promoted today: 71 PARTIAL to MAPPED.** **No functional tests performed and no PRD deployment.**

### O06 corrective source provenance and nested UClass fields (2026-10-23)

**Architectural correction:** Unreal v1.227 is NOT UT99. Both can use archive version 69, but game identity cannot be inferred from that number. Previous CatalogUE1ClassicSourceFamily automatically chose UT99 for package versions 62–69; this was wrong and has been removed. The new selector requires an explicit UT99 loader source for otherwise unidentified later UE1 archives; it automatically recognizes Unreal v1.227 only from the pinned version 69 / licensee 227 signature, or from explicit v1.227 loader context for compatible older packages. Top-level class, tagged property and native readers select the same profile and pass it into nested class defaults, inherited fields, arrays, structs and scripts. Unresolved lineage returns an error instead of speculative UT99 data. Unreal v1.227's exact UnrealScript Emitter package class identities are XBeamEmitter, XParticleEmitter and XRopeDeco (native C++ classes carry an A prefix); the game overlay now uses source package ClassIndex identity, not the wrong C++-prefixed archive names. The common UE1 tagged UObject serialization runs first. The available v1.227 Core/Src implementation is incomplete and its unverified core changes are NOT silently replaced by UT99 source rules.

**Additional UClass implementation:** CatalogUE1ClassicStructArrayValueReader now recursively follows source-defined UStructProperty::SerializeItem -> UStruct::SerializeBin for nested struct member descriptors, preserving exact linker references and ArrayDim order. Cyclic/unresolved layouts remain explicit, and a struct with no persistent fields correctly consumes zero bytes. These improvements are shared by class default structs and array inner structs. **Mapped 252 / PARTIAL 2 / EXCLUDED 38; the two UClass source-contract entries remain genuinely open** for unsupported item types and source-incomplete runtime value semantics. Functional/native verification and PRD deployment deferred.

Cross-generation architectural contract: specs/engine-game-source-precedence.md (also linked in AGENTS.md). It is required for UE2/2.5, UE3, UE4 and UE5 going forward, not a claim that those readers have been fully changed now.

### O06 game-source version eligibility gates (2026-10-23)

Implemented source-defined loader-version gates in CatalogUE1ClassicSourceFamily: Unreal v1.200 34-61, UT99 v400 60-68, Unreal v1.227 60-69, inclusive. Explicit source choice outside its complete-backward-compatibility range is rejected. The CLI presents eligible candidates; overlapping v60-61 (three) and v62-68 (two) do not silently select a game. v69 has only the pinned Unreal v1.227 profile, but version alone is not proof of the producing game, unlike source-proven version/licensee 69/227. Confirmed three local Core/Inc/UnObjVer.h sources and Unreal/UT99 Core/Src/UnLinker.h: older-than-minimum invokes a Yes/No warning rather than unconditional rejection. Strict gates describe complete compatibility rather than every package the engine may attempt to load. O06 register remains 252 MAPPED, 2 PARTIAL, 38 EXCLUDED; no native/corpus tests or PRD deploy.

Corrected UE1 loader source-gate behavior: previously select() rejected explicit game-source choices outside [PACKAGE_MIN_VERSION, PACKAGE_FILE_VERSION], but Unreal v1.200 and UT99 v400 ULinkerLoad::LoadSummary actually warns and requests user approval when loading below the minimum; it does not reject newer versions in that version check. Exact source version diagnostics now separate advertised compatible ranges, below-minimum warnings, above-saver versions, and the unverified v1.227 native linker. No game identity is guessed from version. Also implemented Epic v1.200 UStringProperty FArchive::String field decoder inside nested UStruct::SerializeBin, including bounded ASCII/ANSI bytes and generated NUL when no terminating byte is archived. This closes another UClass default-value codec but both C98/T99 UClass implementations remain PARTIAL pending outstanding field kinds/imported class semantics; register 252 MAPPED / 2 PARTIAL / 38 EXCLUDED. PHP syntax only, no functional tests, no PRD deployment.

Implemented two more source-defined UT99 nested UClass default value codecs in the shared UStruct::SerializeBin field-order reader: UArrayProperty::SerializeItem reads AR_INDEX element count followed by each Inner->SerializeItem, while UFixedArrayProperty::SerializeItem uses the declared Count with no count prefix. Each Inner declaration must be a genuine local linker child of its UArrayProperty or UFixedArrayProperty and its native UProperty serializer must decode correctly. Supported Inner primitives, name/object linker references, FString and recursive UStruct members reuse existing bounded source codecs. ### O06 UT99 v432 supersedes v400 as the single supported UT99 loader (2026-10-23)

The UT99 source profile is now **ut99-v432**, compatible-range metadata 60-69 from v432 Core/Inc/UnObjVer.h (ENGINE_VERSION 430). The old CLI spelling ut99-v400 is a deprecated alias to the v432 target, **not a second source profile**. Existing v400 Core/Engine serializer implementations remain the inherited fallback only where v432's public-source distribution omits method bodies; this is not evidence that v432 runtime serialization is unchanged. Both UE1 original baseline and game-specific UT99 modifications are preserved separately. v432 Core/Inc has 28 changed and 6 new public headers; Engine/Inc has 37 changed and 11 new, documented in UE1-UT99-V432-SOURCE-DELTA.json. The published v432 distribution does not contain its native Core/Src or Engine/Src, supplying precompiled libraries instead. Confirmed new v432 skeletal class declarations: USkeletalMesh and UAnimation, plus source-defined FMeshExtWedge 12-byte serialization. The production V432NativeDeltaReader implements exact FMeshExtWedge record decoding and source-bounded original opaque native-tail preservation, while explicitly refusing to guess whole-class byte order. Both newly discovered native class methods are registered as two **PARTIAL** obligations. All inherited T99 rows are marked v432 native-oracle **OPEN**, rather than falsely accepted on the basis of v400 source alone. **Current register 254 MAPPED / 2 PARTIAL / 38 EXCLUDED; O06 NOT G5 ACCEPTED.** Mapping totals prior to this review described the older pinned v400 source set, not a v432 complete-engine claim. No native test campaign or PRD deployment.

### O06 UT99 v432 compiled skeletal/animation methods and UFont delta (2026-10-23)

SHA-pinned genuine installed v432 Engine.dll contains complete native implementations, unlike the Engine.lib import stubs. Native method disassembly gives UAnimation::Serialize UObject plus arrays RefBones, Moves, AnimSeqs; USkeletalMesh::Serialize ULodMesh followed by six skeletal arrays and SkeletalDepth, DefaultAnimation, WeaponBoneIndex and WeaponAdjust in compiled order, not declaration order. Production V432NativeDeltaReader implements both, along with FNamedBone, FMeshBone, VJointPos, VBoneInfIndex, VBoneInfluence, FMeshExtWedge, MotionChunk, AnalogTrack, and FMeshAnimSeq. VJointPos serializes XSize twice, not YSize; FMeshBone omits Depth. An undefined fullRadius reference in inherited ULodMesh bounds was corrected for the selected UT99 source and archive version. Three targeted DEV source-shaped fixture checks passed, including bounded malformed input tests and entire UAnimation 60-byte and USkeletalMesh 379-byte archive fixtures. Thus both new v432 methods are MAPPED for file serialization, not genuine runtime loaded-value G5 acceptance. The compiled v432 UFont::Serialize at method RVA 0x846D0 has a real newer v69 change: CharRemap and IsRemapped after existing fields. The inherited v400 font reader does not decode them. Reopen T99:UFont::Serialize as PARTIAL pending exact map codec/ABI and compiled comparison. **Register 255 MAPPED, 1 PARTIAL, 38 EXCLUDED.** Native source evidence and SHA: UE1-UT99-V432-COMPILED-SERIALIZERS.json. No PRD deployment or broad corpus rerun.

### O06 v432 UFont CharRemap final file-archive implementation (2026-10-23)

Completed the remaining v432 UFont source-method mismatch using compiled Engine.dll UFont::Serialize RVA 0x846D0 and TMap pair serializer RVA 0x84D00. Original v432 header specifies TMap<TCHAR,TCHAR> CharRemap, IsRemapped UBOOL; compiled v69 branch archives compact pair count, two 16-bit characters per pair (4 wire bytes vs 8 bytes in native TPair memory), then 32-bit IsRemapped. Older versions set IsRemapped=0 without extra persisted bytes. Implemented both branches in production v432 font reader with bounded counts, source CHAR indices, raw flag and byte hashes. Focused positive/negative DEV fixture passes including truncated maps and legacy/no-tail cases. UFont returns to MAPPED (file methods only). **Updated register MAPPED 256 / PARTIAL 0 / EXCLUDED 38.** All 3 v432-discovered native file serializers (UAnimation, USkeletalMesh, UFont remap) are now source/compiled-method mapped; G5 genuine source-to-runtime equivalence of inherited methods and actual skeletal/animation/font loaded values is still OPEN. No PRD deployment or broad native test campaign.

Absence of an old compiled oracle is an evidence limitation, not a reason to refuse implementation of available source contracts. **Do not declare O06 accepted by excluding required class/script/level/mesh serializers, and do not invent new acceptance gates without a named source branch that fits O06.**

**Execution order:** close G1 and G2 by short read-only source-to-handler inventories (avoid re-running passing fixtures); then implement G3 and G4 in source-grouped batches, with per-test durable logging; finally G5 and G6. Update this 6-row board after each gate, not the endless historical checkpoint paragraphs.

**O06 classic FPropertyTag stream termination correction (2026-10-23):** The shared production `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader::parseTags` previously invented `MAX_TAGS=20000` and would reject a syntactically valid source tag archive with more than 20,000 tags. This fixes actual valid large-file compatibility without a guessed viewer cap. **O06 remains PARTIAL/NOT ACCEPTED**, and no PRD PHP execution was performed.

**O06 T99 archived fixed-string -> dynamic string compatibility (2026-10-23):** Actual Epic UT99 v400 `Core/Src/UnClass.cpp::UStruct::SerializeTaggedProperties` has a distinct legacy load conversion for a serialized `NAME_StringProperty` tag whose current declaration is `NAME_StrProperty`. The branch calls `FromAnsi` into `TCHAR Temp[255]` and assigns an `FString` **before** normal type mismatch/array bounds and `ShouldSerializeValue` checks. Previous UnrealDB `CatalogUE1ClassicClassDefaultSchemaReader` misreported this case as type mismatch and safe skipped it; classic `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader` also left old StringProperty bytes opaque for >=62. **O06 C98 vs T99 historical struct-name alias correction (2026-10-23):** Epic C98 Unreal v1.200 `Core/Src/UnClass.cpp::UStruct::SerializeTaggedProperties` explicitly rewrites old on-disk `StructProperty` ItemName `Rotation`→`Rotator` and `Region`→`PointRegion` on load. **O06 T99 FString source-counted ANSI/Unicode archive decoding (2026-10-23):** Genuine pinned UT99 v400 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UStrProperty::SerializeItem` archives `FString`, and actual `Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp::operator<<(FArchive&,FString&)` persists signed **compact `SaveNum`**. Positive count is the number of serialized ANSI single bytes, negative count its negated 16-bit Unicode code-unit count. This includes terminal NUL; `SaveNum==0` is empty and load-time `A.Num()==1` empties a lone terminator. The prior shared `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader` returned `StrProperty` tagged values opaque even for perfectly valid UT99 archives, and class-schema matching could misleadingly report a declaration match without distinguishing a malformed dynamic-string value. Production now source-parses both ANSI/UTF-16LE archives strictly within the real tag-size range, checks count equals byte payload, terminal NUL, preserves exact original bytes as hex and exposes **only safe ASCII-derived text previews**. Locale-dependent `FromAnsi`/`FromUnicode` and native `FString` heap allocation remain **UNVERIFIED**, never simulated or mislabeled as completed engine effects. `CatalogUE1ClassicClassDefaultSchemaReader` flags malformed counted dynamic-string values as `dynamic_fstring_archive_unverified`, not silently verified. New complete synthetic v69 `.u` package and actual native UStrProperty declaration full-path regression `verify-ue1-classic-fstring-archive.php` **59/59 DEV PASS** covers ANSI, UTF-16LE, non-ASCII byte preservation, count 0 and 1 empty, >255 byte tagged payload, bad count/truncation and missing NUL. Eleven affected DEV scripts **11/11 PASS**, including all relevant genuine nontexture/class-default and 92-file classic corpora. There is **no matching genuine compiled UT99 v69 StrProperty class-default corpus or compiled runtime oracle** in this batch; T99 source rows remain PARTIAL, O06 remains IMPLEMENTING / NOT ACCEPTED. Only DEV tests, PRD deployment hash-only.

**UE1 O06 — UT99 native `ArrayProperty` primitive/identity values (2026-10-23):** Genuine Epic UT99 v400 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UArrayProperty::SerializeItem` writes/reads `AR_INDEX(n)` then iterates `Inner->SerializeItem` for each element; it does **not** assume an array's element type from its raw byte size. For T99-scoped v>=62 class-default arrays the reader uses the originating tag archive for the value and linker maps, and the owning class/superclass source package for the declaration. Source-typed inner `ByteProperty` (BYTE), `IntProperty` (signed INT32), `FloatProperty` (IEEE754 including raw hex on nonfinite), `BoolProperty` (BYTE, any nonzero true), `NameProperty` (compact FName map index) and `ObjectProperty` (signed compact import/export/null index) are handled; source-array count/entire payload length and existing tag value SHA-256 checked, invalid object/name linker indices fatal, and full sequence parsed with bounded 32-item preview. Unsupported `StrProperty`/struct/nested types, negative count, truncated inner compact ref, mismatched count/payload and trailing bytes are **explicitly unverified**, not fabricated as decoded. Tests run only on DEV; PRD receives SHA-matched deployed files without redundant PHP tests.

**UE1 O06 — UT99 `UFixedArrayProperty` byte-correct serialization (2026-10-23):** Epic v400 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UFixedArrayProperty::SerializeItem` **does not archive an element-count prefix**; it loops exactly the signed `Count` stored in native `UFixedArrayProperty::Serialize`, using its native `Inner` field and `Inner->SerializeItem` on each element. **UE1 O06 — UT99 nested FString arrays share Epic archive codec (2026-10-23):** Pinned UT99 v400 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UStrProperty::SerializeItem` calls `Ar << *(FString*)Value`; `UArrayProperty::SerializeItem` serializes `AR_INDEX(n)` and calls `Inner->SerializeItem` for each element, whereas `UFixedArrayProperty::SerializeItem` uses persisted native `Count` with no count prefix. `Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp::operator<<(FArchive&,FString&)` writes signed compact ANSI (+N byte count) or 16-bit Unicode (−N code-unit count) including terminator. Introduced shared production `CatalogUE1ClassicFStringArchiveReader::decodeAt`, replacing duplicate counted-string parsing in `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader`; the same codec now decodes independent ANSI, Unicode, empty, non-ASCII and >255-byte `StrProperty` elements in both dynamic and fixed arrays via actual `UProperty.Inner` source references in `CatalogUE1ClassicArrayValueReader`. It validates count/width/terminator/physical bounds, preserves original hex, only claims ASCII text when safe, keeps unsupported runtime `TCHAR` conversion and allocation unverified. It fully traverses every element but retains **only 32 preview values**, demonstrated with 40-element dynamic/fixed fixtures. Truncated nested Unicode, missing NUL and trailing data fail closed as explicitly unverified. `verify-ue1-classic-array-inner-values.php` **154/154 DEV PASS** (latest event 20261023T103029593Z-a545fe94), including explicit pinned `UnProp.cpp`/`UnMisc.cpp` assertions and source SHA. `verify-ue1-classic-fstring-archive.php` **59/59 DEV PASS** after refactor; twelve affected DEV regressions PASS and one identical already-journaled PASS skipped; authentic 92-file and 16-package UT99 corpora unaffected. **Do not infer nested `UStructProperty` values from names**: Epic `UStructProperty::SerializeItem` calls `Struct->SerializeBin`, which requires actual `UProperty` declaration graph and field order. Nested struct semantics and genuine compiled engine parity remain OPEN, O06 NOT ACCEPTED. All tests DEV only; no PRD PHP execution. UE5 probe folders untouched.

**UE1 O06 — UT99 field-based nested UStruct values (2026-10-23):** Epic v400 Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UStructProperty::SerializeItem actually invokes Struct->SerializeBin after Ar.Preload(Struct). Core/Src/UnClass.cpp::UStruct::SerializeBin traverses TFieldIterator<UProperty> in source Children / UField.Next order, tests ShouldSerializeValue (CPF_Native and persistent CPF_Transient), then calls each member SerializeItem for its actual ArrayDim. New production CatalogUE1ClassicStructArrayValueReader, linked from CatalogUE1ClassicArrayValueReader, resolves an actual local native-complete UStruct export through UStructProperty.Struct and validates real UProperty child export ownership, chain, type and array dimensions. It serializes Byte/Bool/signed-Int/Float/UTF counted FString members in actual field order, skipping persistent transient/native members, traverses all serialized bytes with SHA/source bounds, and retains at most 32 previewed values per member and per array. ## Mandatory source-first execution and acceptance discipline (2026-10-21)

**Policy correction after repeated source-coverage misses and O01 scope expansion:**

1. **Inventory source before interpreting or editing a format.** For each engine, recursively enumerate ALL supplied engine/game source roots and relevant C++ load/save code; search format tags, headers AND EOF trailers, version gates, serializers, loader functions, and all source revisions. Register the exact source file, symbol/line and branch difference. The absent `UnObjVer.h` is NOT evidence of absent source: original Unreal v0.82–0.84a use `UnRes.cpp` and trailer metadata, not the later `ULinker`.
2. **No unsupported "source gap" assumptions.** Before calling information unavailable, search alternative file names, legacy subsystems, adjacent versions, engine and game trees, actual loader/writer implementations, and compiled artifacts where useful; record searches and distinguish (a) source genuinely absent for a particular fork, (b) UnrealDB reader not yet built, and (c) independent test fixture not yet available. Treat source definitions as implementation authority; do not delay source-driven work pending an optional old executable.
3. **Freeze a narrow operation contract BEFORE coding.** O01 is file-format identification, version/licensee extraction, selected-game dispatch and honest unsupported/invalid results. Full name/import/export decoding is O02–O04; dependencies O05; payloads O06/O07; production SQL/search O09; complete workflow acceptance O10. Later operation failures must not block earlier acceptance unless they actually change its inputs or outputs.
4. **Work each operation in one bounded source→diff→fix→accept sequence:** exhaustive source mapping once, real production caller coverage, minimal correction, source-anchored positive/negative tests, standard regressions, evidence of observed differences, Git commit/push and immediate PRD SHA-256 deployment. Never add arbitrary B-number cycles or new acceptance conditions after work begins without a demonstrated scope error.
5. **Publish measurable results:** per-operation PASS/FAIL and exact open acceptance checks; genuine corpus and regression counts separately; source findings with true status (source present, code absent, or test missing). Do not conflate tests passed with engine accepted. After closing O01, **advance to O02** without reopening O01 for unrelated improvements.

The source-first rule is mandatory for UE1 and repeats unchanged for UE2/2.5, UE3/UDK, UE4 and UE5. Review all supplied code branches before stating that any engine information is missing.

## G3-03 original C98 Class Object source-runtime ScriptText context resolution (2026-10-27)

A NEW genuine original May 17 1998 retail Core.dll native CDB investigation isolates the first C98 non-byte-for-byte pointer observation missed by script-only comparisons: in authentic version61 Core.u the class export 0 `Object` stores signed compact `ScriptText=231` (export230 `TextBuffer2`, ObjectFlags `0x00340000`) and `Children=457` (export456 `EndState`, flags `0x00070004`). The original shipped x86 `UStruct::Serialize` method actually executed on Class Object, and its archive-load instructions `10110b47`/ `10110b5d` were captured before and after the two pointer operator calls: native `ScriptText` stays `NULL` (0 -> 0), while native `Children` becomes non-NULL (0 -> 0x0513df10 in the observed run). The genuine original archived references were NOT NULL. These facts are not an UnrealDB archive bug: its class reader deliberately preserves original saved references and explicitly reports `runtime_refs_resolved=false`, rather than pretending to be Epic's in-process linker.

The separate May 19 v1.200 Epic source `Core/Src/UnLinker.h` (SHA-256 `82008da9b50ba09f8268b52d07f8d9e1fc2333ea35b229dcff7c30a521486b1f`) constructs runtime `ContextFlags` from GIsEditor/GIsClient/GIsServer, uses `IndexToObject` on every saved UObject index, and `CreateExport` returns NULL when `Export.ObjectFlags & ContextFlags == 0`. Epic `Core/Inc/UnObjBas.h` (SHA-256 `6f1668c2ec156a302f99f8a7ae6e1161bac1706e551d70ed9445ab9ae55c68f7`) sets `RF_LoadForServer=0x00020000`, `RF_LoadForClient=0x00010000` and `RF_LoadForEdit=0x00040000`. Actual `TextBuffer2` flags have neither server nor client bit but do have edit, whereas actual `EndState` flags include all three; this source context filtering predicts the observed genuine compiled 1998 server-side null/non-null distinction. May 17 compiled binary is NOT claimed byte-identical to the later source snapshot; the runtime observation is independently from the original retail binary.

Reproducible NEW CDB original-native evidence: `catalog/compliance/evidence/o06/20261027-C98-CoreObject-ScriptText-CONTEXT-CDB.txt` (SHA-256 `0c6e7420073906de66904282daa70b836e29ccc67ad6476a533c1a95d4b6218d`), commands `20261027-C98-CoreObject-ScriptText-CONTEXT-COMMAND.txt` (SHA-256 `89b28cfb6bbf76a41bd0ef528e05c74602387708f0322279d60da5085d08fe19`). Original local Core.dll/Core.u full hashes remain unchanged. A temporary prior correct 1998 CD N: mount was removed after its previous certified run; original `Main.cpp` requires only `Textures\\Palettes.utx` for CD presence. The isolated original C98 sandbox `Default.ini` and `Unreal.ini` now point to user's mounted `O:` disc, which also contains that file; untouched original binaries and genuine installed package bytes continued to execute.
